So the next step is to have Ava ask Mia if she checked the drawer. Then based on the answer, Ava decides. But since the user wants the assistant to generate Ava's response, in Turn 1, Ava would ask that question. Then in the next turn, Mia would respond, and then Ava would decide. But in this case, the user is providing the initial Turn 0 and Turn 1, where Turn 1 is Ava's response.
